```inc
      data = FindData(world, types[t], f[3], error);
    if (!data) {
      out = error;
      return true;
    }
    const std::string sectionName = Option(f, 4, "section", "");
    InventorySection *section = sectionName.empty() ? nullptr : inv->getSection(sectionName);
    if (!sectionName.empty() && !Valid(section)) {
      out = b->getName() + " has no section " + sectionName;
      return true;
    }
    const int before = CountAllSections(inv, data);
    error.clear();
    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
      Item *item = MakeItem(world, data, f, 4, nullptr, error);
      if (!item)
        break;
      bool placed = section ? section->addItem(item, 1) : inv->addItem(item, 1, false, true);
      if (!placed)
        break;
    }
    const int after = CountAllSections(inv, data);
    ok = after > before;
    Log("KAH: fill " + b->getName() + " " + data->name + " " + Int(before) + " -> " + Int(after));
    out = (ok ? "" : (error.empty() ? "nothing fitted: " : error + ": ")) + b->getName() + " " +
          data->name + " " + Int(before) + " -> " + Int(after) + " (" + Int(after - before) + " of " +
          Int(count) + " fitted)";
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

// Body part by index (0..6) or name.
int ParsePart(const std::string &text) {
  static const char *const names[] = {"head", "chest", "stomach", "left_arm", "right_arm",
                                      "left_leg", "right_leg"};
  std::string t = Lower(text);
  t.erase(std::remove(t.begin(), t.end(), ' '), t.end());
  for (int i = 0; i < 7; ++i) {
    std::string joined = names[i];
    joined.erase(std::remove(joined.begin(), joined.end(), '_'), joined.end());
    if (t == names[i] || t == joined)
      return i;
  }
  char *end = nullptr;
  long n = strtol(text.c_str(), &end, 10);
  return (!text.empty() && end && !*end && n >= 0 && n < 7) ? (int)n : -1;
}

struct ShopSource {
  std::string name;
  Inventory *inv;
};

void InventoryItems(Inventory *inv, std::vector<Item *> &out) {
  if (!Valid(inv))
    return;
  lektor<InventorySection *> &sections = inv->getAllSections();
  for (uint32_t si = 0; si < sections.size(); ++si) {
    InventorySection *section = sections.stuff[si];
    if (!Valid(section))
      continue;
    const Ogre::vector<InventorySection::SectionItem>::type &items = section->getItems();
    for (size_t k = 0; k < items.size(); ++k)
      if (Valid(items[k].item))
        out.push_back(items[k].item);
  }
}

// A trader's goods: her own inventory, plus storage in her shop (buildings
// within `radius` of her that belong to her faction: Kenshi keeps shop stock
// there; traders walk around their shops, so the default is 60).
void CollectShopSources(GameWorld *world, Character *trader, float radius,
                        std::vector<ShopSource> &out) {
  ShopSource own = {trader->getName() + " (carried)", trader->getInventory()};
  out.push_back(own);
  Faction *fac = trader->getFaction();
  lektor<RootObject *> nearby;
  world->getObjectsWithinSphere(nearby, trader->getPosition(), radius, BUILDING, 256, nullptr);
  for (uint32_t i = 0; i < nearby.size(); ++i) {
    Building *b = dynamic_cast<Building *>(nearby.stuff[i]);
    if (!Valid(b))
      continue;
    try {
      if (b->getFaction() != fac || !Valid(b->getInventory()))
        continue;
      ShopSource src = {b->getName(), b->getInventory()};
      out.push_back(src);
    } catch (...) {
    }
  }
}

// World commands about one character (f[2] is the character, already resolved as c).
bool RunCharacterCommand(GameWorld *world, const std::vector<std::string> &f, Character *c,
                         const Ogre::Vector3 &origin, bool &ok, std::string &out) {
  const std::string cmd = Lower(f[1]);
  if (cmd == "drop") { // drop <npc> <item> [count]: put an inventory item on the ground (KAH 15)
    if (f.size() < 4) {
      out = "usage: drop <npc> <item> [count]";
      return true;
    }
    bool ambiguous = false;
    Item *item = FindInventoryItem(c, f[3], ambiguous);
    if (!item) {
      out = ambiguous ? "several items match '" + f[3] + "': use the full name or sid"
                      : c->getName() + " carries no " + f[3];
      return true;
    }
    if (f.size() >= 5 && atoi(f[4].c_str()) != item->quantity) {
      out = "drop takes the whole stack: " + item->getName() + " x" + Int(item->quantity) +
            " (give count " + Int(item->quantity) + " or none)";
      return true;
    }
    if (item->isEquipped) {
      out = item->getName() + " is worn: unequip it first";
      return true;
    }
    Inventory *inv = c->getInventory();
    const bool pendingBefore = item->creatingPhysical || item->physicalShouldExist;
    try {
      inv->dropItem(item);
    } catch (...) {
    }
    ok = Valid(item) && !item->isInInventory && PlacedOrDropping(item, pendingBefore);
    if (ok) {
      g_dropped.push_back(item->getHandle());
      if (g_dropped.size() > 64)
        g_dropped.erase(g_dropped.begin());
    }
    Log("KAH: drop " + c->getName() + " " + item->getName() + " ok=" + (ok ? "1" : "0"));
    out = std::string(ok ? "dropped " : "drop failed: ") + GroundItemLine(item, c->getPosition()) +
          (ok && !item->_isPhysical ? " (ground object still being built)" : "");
    return true;
  }
  if (cmd == "sever") { // sever <npc> <limb> [noitem] [ko]: the game's own amputation (KAH 20)
    static const char *const limbNames[] = {"left_arm", "right_arm", "left_leg", "right_leg"};
    static const char *const states[] = {"original", "stump", "replaced", "crushed"};
    int limb = -1;
    const std::string want = f.size() >= 4 ? Lower(f[3]) : "";
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i)
      if (want == limbNames[i])
        limb = i;
    if (limb < 0) {
      out = "usage: sever <npc> left_arm|right_arm|left_leg|right_leg [noitem] [ko]";
      return true;
    }
    bool makeItem = true, knockout = false;
    for (size_t i = 4; i < f.size(); ++i) {
      if (Lower(f[i]) == "noitem")
        makeItem = false;
      if (Lower(f[i]) == "ko")
        knockout = true;
    }
    MedicalSystem &med = c->medical;
    const RobotLimbs::Limb l = (RobotLimbs::Limb)limb;
    const int before = (int)med.getLimbState(l);
    if (before == LIMB_STUMP || before == LIMB_CRUSHED) {
      out = c->getName() + " " + limbNames[limb] + " is already lost (" + states[before] + ")";
      return true;
    }
    med.amputate(l, makeItem, Ogre::Vector3::ZERO);
    // As after a real cut-off (and Stobe's own amputation): the limb part
    // reads as destroyed.
    MedicalSystem::HealthPartStatus *part =
        limb == 0 ? med.leftArm : limb == 1 ? med.rightArm : limb == 2 ? med.leftLeg : med.rightLeg;
    if (Valid(part)) {
      part->flesh = -100.0f;
      if (part->fleshStun > -100.0f)
        part->fleshStun = -100.0f;
      part->updateDerivedHealths();
    }
    if (knockout)
      med.knockoutForceTimer(8.0f);
    const int after = (int)med.getLimbState(l);
    ok = after == LIMB_STUMP || after == LIMB_CRUSHED;
    Log("KAH: sever " + c->getName() + " " + limbNames[limb] + " state " + Int(before) + " -> " + Int(after));
    out = c->getName() + " " + limbNames[limb] + " limb_state " +
          (before >= 0 && before < 4 ? states[before] : "?") + " -> " +
          (after >= 0 && after < 4 ? states[after] : "?") +
          (makeItem ? " (severed limb item dropped)" : "") + (knockout ? " KO 8 s" : "") +
          (ok ? "" : " (the game did not mark it lost)");
    return true;
  }
  if (cmd == "pickup") { // pickup <npc> <item|#serial/index|nearest> [radius <m>]: ground item into inventory (KAH 15)
    if (f.size() < 4) {
      out = "usage: pickup <npc> <item name|sid|#serial/index|nearest> [radius <m>]";
      return true;
    }
    Ogre::Vector3 center = c->getPosition();
    const std::string nearName = Option(f, 4, "near", "");
    if (!nearName.empty()) {
      Character *nc = FindCharacter(world, nearName);
      float bd = 0;
      Building *nb = nc ? nullptr : FindBuilding(world, origin, nearName, kDefaultSearchRadius, bd);
      if (nc)
        center = nc->getPosition();
      else if (nb)
        center = nb->getPosition();
    }
    float radius = (float)atof(Option(f, 4, "radius", nearName.empty() ? "20" : "30").c_str());
    if (!(radius > 0) || radius > 300)
      radius = 20;
    std::string why;
    Item *item = FindGroundItem(world, center, f[3], radius, why);
    if (!item) {
      out = why;
      return true;
    }
    const hand before = item->getHandle();
    const std::string itemName = item->getName();
    const int qty = item->quantity;
    const Ogre::Vector3 itemPos = item->getPosition();
    Inventory *inv = c->getInventory();
    bool given = false;
    try {
      given = c->giveItem(item, false, false);
    } catch (...) {
      given = false;
    }
    // A stackable item can merge into a stack he has and be destroyed: only
    // touch the instance while his inventory still holds it.
    // Still the same object? Resolve the old handle again (no pointer use
    // before that): a pack can go straight onto a free back slot, which the
    // inventory's item list doesn't hold, and that is not a stack merge.
    Item *again = nullptr;
    try {
      again = before.getItem();
    } catch (...) {
      again = nullptr;
    }
    const bool alive = again == item && Valid(again);
    if (alive && item->isEquipped) {
      ok = true;
      Log("KAH: pickup " + c->getName() + " " + itemName + " equipped to " + item->inventorySection);
      out = "picked up " + itemName + " x" + Int(qty) + " equipped to " + item->inventorySection + " " +
            FormatCharacterRef(before.serial, before.index) + " (same instance)";
      return true;
    }
    const bool held = alive && (item->isInInventory || (Valid(inv) && inv->hasItem(item)));
    if (given && !alive) {
      ok = true;
      Log("KAH: pickup " + c->getName() + " " + itemName + " merged into a stack");
      out = "picked up " + itemName + " x" + Int(qty) + " (merged into a stack: not the same instance)";
      return true;
    }
    ok = held;
    std::string how = "giveItem=" + std::string(given ? "1" : "0");
    if (!ok && !given) {
      // The game's own way: walk over and pick it up.
      OrdersReceiver *orders = c->getOrdersReciever();
      if (Valid(orders)) {
        orders->addOrder(PICKUP, before, itemPos, true, false);
        how += " -> ordered PICKUP (check inv after the walk)";
      }
    }
    const hand after = item->getHandle();
    Log("KAH: pickup " + c->getName() + " " + itemName + " ok=" + (ok ? "1" : "0") + " " + how);
    out = std::string(ok ? "picked up " : "not picked up yet: ") + itemName + " x" + Int(qty) +
          " handle " + FormatCharacterRef(before.serial, before.index) + " -> " +
          FormatCharacterRef(after.serial, after.index) +
          (before.serial == after.serial ? " (same instance)" : " (handle changed)") +
          " in_inventory=" + (item->isInInventory ? "1" : "0") + " " + how;
    return true;
  }
  if (cmd == "order") { // order <npc> <task> [target <npc>] [building <name>] [keep]
    if (f.size() < 4) {
      out = "usage: order <npc> <task name|number> [target <npc>] [building <name>] [keep]";
      return true;
    }
    TaskType task;
    if (!ParseTask(f[3], task)) {
      out = "unknown task: " + f[3] + " (tasks <filter> lists them)";
      return true;
    }
    RootObject *target = nullptr;
    const std::string targetName = Option(f, 4, "target", "");
    const std::string buildingName = Option(f, 4, "building", "");
    Character *targetChar = nullptr;
    if (!targetName.empty()) {
      targetChar = FindCharacter(world, targetName);
      if (!targetChar) {
        out = NotFound(targetName);
        return true;
      }
      target = targetChar;
    } else if (!buildingName.empty()) {
      float dist = 0;
      target = FindBuildingT<Building>(world, c->getPosition(), buildingName, 300.0f, dist);
      if (!target) {
        out = "no building matching '" + buildingName + "' within 300 of " + c->getName();
        return true;
      }
    }
    bool keep = false;
    for (size_t i = 4; i < f.size(); ++i)
      if (Lower(f[i]) == "keep")
        keep = true;
    OrdersReceiver *orders = c->getOrdersReciever();
    if (!Valid(orders)) {
      out = c->getName() + " takes no orders";
      return true;
    }
    if (targetChar)
      RunBeforeAttack(c, targetChar); // harmless for non-attack tasks
    orders->addOrder(task, target ? target->getHandle() : hand(),
                     target ? target->getPosition() : c->getPosition(), !keep, keep);
    ok = true;
    Log("KAH: order " + c->getName() + " " + TaskName(task) +
        (target ? " -> " + target->getName() : std::string()));
    out = c->getName() + " ordered " + TaskName(task) +
          (target ? " -> " + target->getName() : std::string()) +
          " (has_orders=" + (orders->hasOrders() ? "1" : "0") + ")";
    return true;
  }
  if (cmd == "fight") { // fight <a> <b>: make two characters (of different factions) fight
    if (f.size() < 4) {
      out = "usage: fight <npc> <npc2>";
      return true;
    }
    Character *b = FindCharacter(world, f[3]);
    if (!b || b == c) {
      out = "no other character named: " + f[3];
      return true;
    }
    Faction *fa = c->getFaction(), *fb = b->getFaction();
    if (!Valid(fa) || !Valid(fb)) {
      out = "faction not found";
      return true;
    }
    if (fa == fb) {
      out = "same faction (" + fa->getName() + "): move one first with faction <npc> <faction>";
      return true;
    }
    if (Valid(fa->relations))
      fa->relations->setRelation(fb, -100.0f);
    if (Valid(fb->relations))
      fb->relations->setRelation(fa, -100.0f);
    Character *pair[2][2] = {{c, b}, {b, c}};
    for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
      RunBeforeAttack(pair[i][0], pair[i][1]);
      OrdersReceiver *orders = pair[i][0]->getOrdersReciever();
      if (Valid(orders))
        orders->addOrder(UNPROVOKED_FOCUSED_MELEE_ATTACK, pair[i][1]->getHandle(),
                         pair[i][1]->getPosition(), true, false);
      pair[i][0]->attackTarget(pair[i][1]);
    }
    ok = true;
    Log("KAH: fight " + c->getName() + " <-> " + b->getName());
    out = c->getName() + " [" + fa->getName() + "] and " + b->getName() + " [" + fb->getName() +
          "] fight (factions set to -100)";
    return true;
  }
  if (cmd == "job") { // job <npc> <building> [task <name>] [radius <m>]: add a permanent job
    if (f.size() < 4) {
      out = "usage: job <npc> <building> [task <name>] [radius <m>]";
      return true;
    }
    float radius = 0, dist = 0;
    if (!ParseSearchRadius(f, 4, 0, kDefaultSearchRadius, radius, out))
      return true;
    Building *b = FindBuildingT<Building>(world, c->getPosition(), f[3], radius, dist);
    if (!b) {
      out = "no building matching '" + f[3] + "' within " + Num(radius) + " of " + c->getName();
      return true;
    }
    TaskType task = b->getDefaultTask();
    const std::string taskName = Option(f, 4, "task", "");
    if (!taskName.empty() && !ParseTask(taskName, task)) {
      out = "unknown task: " + taskName;
      return true;
    }
    c->addJob(task, b, true, true, b->getPosition());
    OrdersReceiver *orders = c->getOrdersReciever();
    ok = Valid(orders) && orders->hasPermajob(task);
    Log("KAH: job " + c->getName() + " " + TaskName(task) + " at " + b->getName());
    out = std::string(ok ? "" : "job not recorded: ") + c->getName() + " job " + TaskName(task) +
          " at " + b->getName() + " (" + Num(dist) + " away)";
    return true;
  }
  if (cmd == "jobs") { // jobs <npc>: permanent jobs
    OrdersReceiver *orders = c->getOrdersReciever();
    if (!Valid(orders)) {
      out = c->getName() + " takes no orders";
      return true;
    }
    out = c->getName() + " jobs=" + Int(orders->getPermajobCount()) + ":";
    for (int i = 0; i < orders->getPermajobCount(); ++i)
      out += " [" + TaskName((int)orders->getPermajob(i)) + " " + orders->getPermajobName(i) + "]";
    out += " has_orders=" + std::string(orders->hasOrders() ? "1" : "0");
    ok = true;
    return true;
  }
  if (cmd == "clearjobs") {
    c->clearPermajobs();
    ok = true;
    out = c->getName() + " jobs cleared";
    return true;
  }
  if (cmd == "setname") { // setname <npc> <new name>
    if (f.size() < 4 || f[3].empty()) {
      out = "usage: setname <npc> <new name>";
      return true;
    }
    const std::string before = c->getName();
    c->setName(f[3]);
    ok = c->getName() == f[3];
    Log("KAH: setname " + before + " -> " + c->getName());
    out = before + " -> " + c->getName();
    return true;
  }
  if (cmd == "faction") { // faction <npc> <faction>: move the character (own squad) to a faction
    if (f.size() < 4) {
      out = "usage: faction <npc> <faction name>";
      return true;
    }
    Faction *target = FindFaction(world, f[3]);
    if (!target) {
      out = "no faction named: " + f[3];
      return true;
    }
    Faction *before = c->getFaction();
    Platoon *own = c->separateIntoMyOwnSquad(true);
    if (!Valid(own)) {
      out = "could not separate " + c->getName() + " into an own squad";
      return true;
    }
    own->setFaction(target);
    // The squad's faction alone doesn't reach the member: set it on her too.
    if (c->getFaction() != target)
      c->setFaction(target, Valid(own->getActivePlatoon()) ? own->getActivePlatoon() : c->getPlatoon());
    ok = c->getFaction() == target;
    Log("KAH: faction " + c->getName() + " " + (Valid(before) ? before->getName() : "?") + " -> " +
        (Valid(c->getFaction()) ? c->getFaction()->getName() : "?"));
    // Moving to an own squad gives the character a new handle: report it.
    out = c->getName() + " faction " + (Valid(before) ? before->getName() : "?") + " -> " +
          (Valid(c->getFaction()) ? c->getFaction()->getName() : "?") + " now " + Describe(c, nullptr);
    return true;
  }
  if (cmd == "sleep" || cmd == "wake") { // sleep <npc> [bed <name>] | wake <npc>
    OrdersReceiver *orders = c->getOrdersReciever();
    if (!Valid(orders)) {
      out = c->getName() + " takes no orders";
      return true;
    }
    if (cmd == "wake") {
      orders->clearOrders();
      orders->addOrder(GET_OUT_OF_BED, hand(), c->getPosition(), true, false);
      out = c->getName() + " ordered GET_OUT_OF_BED";
    } else {
      const std::string bed = Option(f, 3, "bed", "");
      if (!bed.empty()) {
        float dist = 0;
        Building *b = FindBuildingT<Building>(world, c->getPosition(), bed, 300.0f, dist);
        if (!b) {
          out = "no building matching '" + bed + "' within 300";
          return true;
        }
        orders->addOrder(USE_BED_ORDER, b->getHandle(), b->getPosition(), true, false);
        out = c->getName() + " ordered USE_BED_ORDER -> " + b->getName();
      } else {
        orders->addOrder(SLEEP_ON_FLOOR, hand(), c->getPosition(), true, false);
        out = c->getName() + " ordered SLEEP_ON_FLOOR";
      }
    }
    ok = true;
    Log("KAH: " + out);
    out += " (in_bed_or_cage=" + Int((int)c->inSomething) + ")";
    return true;
  }
  if (cmd == "damage") { // damage <npc> <part> <cut> [blunt] [pierce]: a real wound (bleeds)
    if (f.size() < 5) {
      out = "usage: damage <npc> <part 0-6|head|chest|stomach|left_arm|right_arm|left_leg|right_leg> "
            "<cut> [blunt] [pierce] [bleed <x>]";
      return true;
    }
    int part = ParsePart(f[3]);
    if (part < 0) {
      out = "unknown body part: " + f[3];
      return true;
    }
    MedicalSystem::HealthPartStatus *p = c->medical.getPart((unsigned __int64)part);
    if (!Valid(p)) {
      out = "no body part " + f[3];
      return true;
    }
    float cut = (float)atof(f[4].c_str());
    float blunt = f.size() >= 6 && Lower(f[5]) != "bleed" ? (float)atof(f[5].c_str()) : 0.0f;
    float pierce = f.size() >= 7 && Lower(f[6]) != "bleed" && Lower(f[5]) != "bleed"
                       ? (float)atof(f[6].c_str())
                       : 0.0f;
    // Damages' 4th value is the bleeding the wound causes (default: as deep as the cut).
    float bleed = (float)atof(Option(f, 5, "bleed", Num(cut)).c_str());
    const float before = p->flesh;
    p->applyDamage(Damages(cut, blunt, pierce, bleed, 0.0f));
    ok = true;
    Log("KAH: damage " + c->getName() + " part=" + Int(part) + " cut=" + Num(cut) + " blunt=" +
        Num(blunt) + " pierce=" + Num(pierce) + " flesh " + Num(before) + " -> " + Num(p->flesh));
    out = c->getName() + " part " + Int(part) + " flesh " + Num(before) + " -> " + Num(p->flesh) +
          " bandaging=" + Num(p->bandaging) + " blood=" + Num(c->medical.blood) + "/" +
          Num(c->medical.getMaxBlood()) + " bleed_rate=" + Num(c->medical.currentBleedRate);
    return true;
  }
  if (cmd == "blood") { // blood <npc> <value | percent%>: set blood (low blood -> collapse)
    if (f.size() < 4) {
      out = "usage: blood <npc> <value|percent%>";
      return true;
    }
    const float maxBlood = c->medical.getMaxBlood();
    const float before = c->medical.blood;
    const std::string v = f[3];
    float value = (float)atof(v.c_str());
    if (!v.empty() && v[v.size() - 1] == '%')
      value = maxBlood * value / 100.0f;
    c->medical.blood = value;
    ok = true;
    Log("KAH: blood " + c->getName() + " " + Num(before) + " -> " + Num(value));
    out = c->getName() + " blood " + Num(before) + " -> " + Num(c->medical.blood) + "/" + Num(maxBlood) +
          " (collapse below " + Num(c->medical.pointOfCollapseBloodloss()) + ")";
    return true;
  }
  if (cmd == "shackle" || cmd == "unshackle") { // shackle <npc> [owner <npc>] | unshackle <npc>
    if (cmd == "unshackle") {
      c->setChainedMode(false, hand());
    } else {
      Character *owner = nullptr;
      const std::string ownerName = Option(f, 3, "owner", "");
      if (!ownerName.empty() && !(owner = FindCharacter(world, ownerName))) {
        out = NotFound(ownerName);
        return true;
      }
      c->setChainedMode(true, owner ? owner->getHandle() : hand());
    }
    ok = (cmd == "shackle") == c->isChainedMode();
    Log("KAH: " + cmd + " " + c->getName() + " chained=" + (c->isChainedMode() ? "1" : "0"));
    out = c->getName() + " chained=" + (c->isChainedMode() ? "1" : "0") +
          " slave_state=" + Int((int)c->isSlave());
    return true;
  }
  if (cmd == "cage" || cmd == "uncage") { // cage <npc> [cage name] | uncage <npc> [cage name]
    float dist = 0;
    const std::string cageName = f.size() >= 4 ? f[3] : "cage";
    UseableStuff *cage = FindBuildingT<UseableStuff>(world, c->getPosition(), cageName, 300.0f, dist);
    if (!cage) {
      out = "no cage matching '" + cageName + "' within 300 of " + c->getName();
      return true;
    }
    if (cmd == "cage") {
      c->teleport(cage->getPosition(), Ogre::Quaternion::IDENTITY);
      c->setPrisonMode(true, cage);
    } else {
      c->setPrisonMode(false, cage);
    }
    ok = (cmd == "cage") == (c->inSomething == IN_PRISON);
    Log("KAH: " + cmd + " " + c->getName() + " " + cage->getName() + " state=" + Int((int)c->inSomething));
    out = c->getName() + " " + cmd + " " + cage->getName() + " (" + Num(dist) +
          " away) state=" + Int((int)c->inSomething) + " (0 nothing, 1 bed, 2 prison)";
    return true;
  }
  if (cmd == "shopstock") { // shopstock <trader> [radius <m>]: goods for sale (own + shop storage)
    float radius = 0;
    if (!ParseSearchRadius(f, 3, 0, kDefaultShopRadius, radius, out, kMaxShopRadius))
      return true;
    std::vector<ShopSource> sources;
    CollectShopSources(world, c, radius, sources);
    out = c->getName() + " trader=" + (c->isATrader() ? "1" : "0") + " money=" + Int(c->getMoney());
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sources.size(); ++i) {
      out += " || " + sources[i].name + ":";
      std::vector<Item *> items;
      InventoryItems(sources[i].inv, items);
      for (size_t k = 0; k < items.size() && k < 40; ++k)
        out += " [" + items[k]->getName() + " x" + Int(items[k]->quantity) + " $" +
               Int(items[k]->getValueSingle(true)) + (items[k]->isEquipped ? " worn" : "") + "]";
    }
    ok = true;
    return true;
  }
  if (cmd == "trade") { // trade <buyer> <trader> <item> [radius <m>]: buy through the game's purchase path
    if (f.size() < 5) {
      out = "usage: trade <buyer> <trader> <item> [radius <m>]";
      return true;
    }
    float radius = 0;
    if (!ParseSearchRadius(f, 5, 0, kDefaultShopRadius, radius, out, kMaxShopRadius))
      return true;
    Character *trader = FindCharacter(world, f[3]);
    if (!trader) {
      out = NotFound(f[3]);
      return true;
    }
    std::vector<ShopSource> sources;
    CollectShopSources(world, trader, radius, sources);
    const std::string wanted = Lower(f[4]);
    Item *item = nullptr;
    Inventory *from = nullptr;
    std::string fromName;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sources.size() && !item; ++i) {
      std::vector<Item *> items;
      InventoryItems(sources[i].inv, items);
      for (size_t k = 0; k < items.size(); ++k) {
        if (items[k]->isEquipped)
          continue;
        const std::string n = Lower(items[k]->getName());
        if (n == wanted || (!item && n.find(wanted) != std::string::npos)) {
          item = items[k];
          from = sources[i].inv;
          fromName = sources[i].name;
          if (n == wanted)
            break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!item) {
      out = trader->getName() + " has no (unworn) " + f[4] + " for sale (shopstock lists the goods)";
      return true;
    }
    const int buyerBefore = c->getMoney(), traderBefore = trader->getMoney();
    const std::string itemName = item->getName();
    GameData *itemData = item->data;
    Inventory *buyerInv = c->getInventory();
    Inventory *traderInv = trader->getInventory();
    // Run m16: a full buyer (the Trader fixture's Shay, room=0 for every item)
    // made buyItem refuse or hand back an item nothing could place, and the
    // reply only said "placed=failed". Say why up front; nothing moves.
    bool buyerRoom = true;
    if (Valid(buyerInv) && itemData) {
      try {
        buyerRoom = buyerInv->hasRoomForItem(itemData);
      } catch (...) {
        buyerRoom = true;
      }
    }
    if (!buyerRoom) {
      Log("KAH: trade " + c->getName() + " <- " + trader->getName() + " (" + fromName + ") " +
          itemName + " refused: buyer has no room");
      out = "trade failed: " + c->getName() + " has no room for " + itemName +
            " (inventory full; nothing bought, no cats moved: free space or use another buyer)";
      return true;
    }
    std::vector<std::string> problems;
    std::string steps; // what happened, in order (reply + harness.log)
    // Shop storage (counters, barrels) is not something the game sells from:
    // its items read value 0 and buyItem there pays nobody (run m9). The
    // trader's own inventory is what the trade window sells, so the item moves
    // there first (the trader "takes it from the shelf"), then the normal
    // purchase runs. If the game still prices it at 0 (run m10: armour made
    // by fill/give), or the move equipped it on the trader, or buyItem
    // refuses, the item is sold by hand: taken out, the buyer pays the
    // fallback price (game value, else average price, else game data value),
    // the trader gets the cats, the item goes to the buyer.
    Inventory *storage = nullptr;
    if (from != traderInv) {
      if (!Valid(traderInv)) {
        out = "trade failed: " + trader->getName() + " has no inventory to sell " + itemName + " from";
        return true;
      }
      Item *moved = from->removeItemDontDestroy_returnsItem(item, 1, true);
      if (!Valid(moved)) {
        out = "trade failed: could not take " + itemName + " out of " + fromName;
        return true;
      }
      if (!traderInv->addItem(moved, 1, false, false)) {
        from->addItem(moved, 1, false, true);
        out = "trade failed: " + trader->getName() + "'s inventory has no room for " + itemName +
              " from " + fromName + " (item left in " + fromName + ")";
        return true;
      }
      storage = from;
      from = traderInv;
      // A stackable item may merge into a stack the trader has: find what
      // is there now (the moved instance if it still exists, else the stack).
      item = nullptr;
      std::vector<Item *> held;
      InventoryItems(traderInv, held);
      for (size_t k = 0; k < held.size(); ++k)
        if (held[k] == moved)
          item = moved;
      for (size_t k = 0; k < held.size() && !item; ++k)
        if (held[k]->data == itemData && !held[k]->isEquipped)
          item = held[k];
      if (!item) {
        out = "trade failed: " + itemName + " vanished after moving it from " + fromName + " to " +
              trader->getName();
        return true;
      }
      steps += " moved " + fromName + " -> " + trader->getName() + " (section '" +
               item->inventorySection + "'" + (item->isEquipped ? ", equipped" : "") + ")";
    }
    int price = item->getValueSingle(true);
    int fallbackPrice = price;
    if (price <= 0) {
      // Why the game says 0: the item's own numbers.
      std::string diag = "the game prices it at 0 (level=" + Int(item->getLevel()) +
                         " quality=" + Num(item->quality) +
                         " material=" + (Valid(item->materialData) ? item->materialData->name : std::string("-")) +
                         " maker=" + (Valid(item->manufacturerData) ? item->manufacturerData->name : std::string("-")) +
                         " stolen=" + (item->isStolen(false) ? "1" : "0");
      const int avg = item->getAvgPrice();
      int dataValue = 0;
      if (Valid(itemData)) {
        try {
          if (itemData->idata.find("value") != itemData->idata.end())
            dataValue = itemData->idata.find("value")->second;
        } catch (...) {
          dataValue = 0;
        }
      }
      diag += " avg_price=" + Int(avg) + " data_value=" + Int(dataValue) + ")";
      problems.push_back(diag);
      fallbackPrice = avg > 0 ? avg : (dataValue > 0 ? dataValue : 0);
    }
    const int countBefore = (Valid(buyerInv) && itemData) ? CountAllSections(buyerInv, itemData) : 0;
    Item *bought = nullptr;
    bool manual = item->isEquipped || price <= 0;
    if (!manual) {
      bought = from->buyItem(item, c);
      if (!Valid(bought)) {
        problems.push_back("buyItem refused (buyer cats " + Int(c->getMoney()) + ", price " + Int(price) + ")");
        manual = true;
      }
    }
    if (manual) {
      if (c->getMoney() < fallbackPrice) {
        problems.push_back("buyer can't afford " + Int(fallbackPrice));
      } else if (Valid(item) && item->isEquipped && !storage) {
        problems.push_back("the trader wears it");
      } else {
        Item *taken = from->removeItemDontDestroy_returnsItem(item, 1, true);
        if (Valid(taken)) {
          c->takeMoney(fallbackPrice);
          trader->takeMoney(-fallbackPrice);
          bought = taken;
          price = fallbackPrice;
          steps += " sold by hand for " + Int(fallbackPrice);
        } else {
          problems.push_back("could not take it out of " + trader->getName() + "'s inventory");
        }
      }
    }
    ok = Valid(bought);
    if (!ok && storage) { // put it back on the shelf
      Item *back = traderInv->removeItemDontDestroy_returnsItem(item, 1, true);
      if (Valid(back) && !storage->addItem(back, 1, false, false))
        traderInv->addItem(back, 1, false, true);
      steps += " returned to " + fromName;
    }
    // buyItem (and the hand sale) leave the item detached (no inventory, not on the ground).
    // Place it the way `give`/`transfer` do (Inventory::addItem, which works for hats where
    // giveItem did not: run m12), then Character::giveItem with drop-at-feet. If neither
    // takes it, the buyer is refunded and the item goes back where it came from.
    std::string placed = ok ? "already" : "-";
    if (ok && !bought->isInInventory && !bought->_isPhysical) {
      const int qty = bought->quantity > 0 ? bought->quantity : 1;
      steps += " placing (qty=" + Int(qty) + " room=" +
               (Valid(buyerInv) && itemData ? (buyerInv->hasRoomForItem(itemData) ? "1" : "0") : "?") +
               " equipped=" + (bought->isEquipped ? "1" : "0") + ")";
      const bool pendingBefore = bought->creatingPhysical || bought->physicalShouldExist;
      bool given = false;
      if (Valid(buyerInv)) {
        try {
          given = buyerInv->addItem(bought, qty, false, false);
        } catch (...) {
          given = false;
        }
        steps += std::string(" addItem=") + (given ? "1" : "0");
      }
      if (!given) {
        // giveItem with dropOnFail drops the item at her feet but still returns false
        // (run m12; Stobe's hand-over ignores its result for the same reason), so the
        // item's own state decides. Last resort: RootObject::dropItem.
        bool r = false;
        try {
          r = c->giveItem(bought, true, false);
        } catch (...) {
          r = false;
        }
        given = PlacedOrDropping(bought, pendingBefore);
        steps += std::string(" giveItem(drop)=") + (r ? "1" : "0") + (given ? " placed" : " not placed");
        if (!given) {
          try {
            c->dropItem(bought);
          } catch (...) {
          }
          given = PlacedOrDropping(bought, pendingBefore);
          steps += std::string(" dropItem=") + (given ? "placed" : "not placed");
        }
      }
      placed = given ? (bought->isInInventory ? "inventory" : "ground") : "failed";
      if (given && !bought->isInInventory && !bought->_isPhysical)
        steps += " (ground object still being built)";
      if (!given) {
        // No money lost: refund what moved and put the item back.
        const int paidNow = buyerBefore - c->getMoney(), receivedNow = trader->getMoney() - traderBefore;
        if (paidNow != 0)
          c->takeMoney(-paidNow);
        if (receivedNow != 0)
          trader->takeMoney(receivedNow);
        Inventory *home = storage ? storage : traderInv;
        bool back = false;
        try {
          back = Valid(home) && home->addItem(bought, qty, false, false);
          if (!back && storage && Valid(traderInv))
            back = traderInv->addItem(bought, qty, false, false);
        } catch (...) {
          back = false;
        }
        steps += " refunded " + Int(paidNow) + (back ? " and put back" : " (item could NOT be put back)");
        problems.push_back("the buyer's inventory refused the item and it could not be dropped; refunded");
        ok = false;
      }
    }
    const int arrived = (Valid(buyerInv) && itemData) ? CountAllSections(buyerInv, itemData) - countBefore : 0;
    const int paid = buyerBefore - c->getMoney(), received = trader->getMoney() - traderBefore;
    if (ok && price > 0 && paid != price)
      problems.push_back("buyer paid " + Int(paid) + " of " + Int(price));
    if (ok && paid > 0 && received != paid)
      problems.push_back("trader received " + Int(received) + " of " + Int(paid));
    if (ok && arrived <= 0 && placed != "ground")
      problems.push_back("nothing arrived in the buyer's inventory");
    std::string why;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < problems.size(); ++i)
      why += std::string(i ? "; " : " problems: ") + problems[i];
    const std::string source = storage ? fromName + " via " + trader->getName() : fromName;
    Log("KAH: trade " + c->getName() + " <- " + trader->getName() + " (" + source + ") " + itemName +
        " ok=" + (ok ? "1" : "0") + " placed=" + placed + " arrived=" + Int(arrived) + " buyer " +
        Int(buyerBefore) + " -> " + Int(c->getMoney()) + " trader " + Int(traderBefore) + " -> " +
        Int(trader->getMoney()) + " steps:" + steps + why);
    out = std::string(ok ? "bought " : "trade failed: ") + itemName + " from " + source +
          " (price " + Int(price) + ") arrived=" + Int(arrived) + " placed=" + placed + ", buyer cats " +
          Int(buyerBefore) + " -> " + Int(c->getMoney()) + ", trader cats " + Int(traderBefore) + " -> " +
          Int(trader->getMoney()) + " steps:" + (steps.empty() ? std::string(" buyItem") : steps) + why;
    return true;
  }
  if (cmd == "eat") { // eat <npc> <food item in inventory>
    if (f.size() < 4) {
      out = "usage: eat <npc> <food item>";
      return true;
    }
    bool ambiguous = false;
    Item *food = FindInventoryItem(c, f[3], ambiguous);
    if (!Valid(food)) {
      out = c->getName() + " has no " + f[3];
      return true;
    }
    const float before = c->medical.hunger * 100.0f;
    ok = c->eatItem(food, c->getInventory());
    out = c->getName() + (ok ? " ate " : " could not eat ") + f[3] + " hunger " + Num(before) +
          " -> " + Num(c->medical.hunger * 100.0f);
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}
```

`tests/build_args_test.cpp`:

```cpp
// Offline test of the "build" arguments (src/BuildArgs.h).
// Build and run: tests\run_tests.bat
#include "BuildArgs.h"

#include <cctype>
#include <cstdio>

std::string Lower(const std::string &value) {
  std::string out = value;
  for (size_t i = 0; i < out.size(); ++i)
    out[i] = (char)tolower((unsigned char)out[i]);
  return out;
}

int g_failed = 0;
void Check(bool cond, const char *what) {
  printf("%s %s\n", cond ? "PASS" : "FAIL", what);
  if (!cond)
    ++g_failed;
}

// "1", "build", then the space-free arguments of `line` (| separates fields).
std::vector<std::string> Args(const std::string &line) {
  std::vector<std::string> f;
  f.push_back("1");
  f.push_back("build");
  size_t start = 0;
  while (start <= line.size()) {
    size_t bar = line.find('|', start);
    if (bar == std::string::npos)
      bar = line.size();
    if (bar > start)
      f.push_back(line.substr(start, bar - start));
    start = bar + 1;
  }
  return f;
}

int main() {
  BuildArgs a;
  std::string err;

  Check(ParseBuildArgs(Args("Bed"), a, err) && a.name == "Bed" && !a.at && a.nearName.empty() &&
            a.dist == 10.0f && a.faction.empty(),
        "name only: near the player, dist 10, player faction");
  Check(ParseBuildArgs(Args("Prisoner Cage|near|Shay|dist|15"), a, err) &&
            a.name == "Prisoner Cage" && a.nearName == "Shay" && a.dist == 15.0f,
        "near npc with dist");
  Check(ParseBuildArgs(Args("Bed|at|100.5|20|-300|faction|Drifters"), a, err) && a.at &&
            a.x == 100.5f && a.y == 20.0f && a.z == -300.0f && a.faction == "Drifters",
        "at x y z with faction");
  Check(ParseBuildArgs(Args("Bed|FACTION|Nameless|NEAR|Malzin"), a, err) &&
            a.faction == "Nameless" && a.nearName == "Malzin",
        "keywords are case-insensitive, any order");

  Check(!ParseBuildArgs(Args(""), a, err) && err.find("usage") != std::string::npos,
        "missing name refused");
  Check(!ParseBuildArgs(Args("Bed|near|Shay|at|1|2|3"), a, err), "near and at together refused");
  Check(!ParseBuildArgs(Args("Bed|at|1|2|x"), a, err), "at with a non-number refused");
  Check(!ParseBuildArgs(Args("Bed|at|1|2"), a, err), "at with two numbers refused");
  Check(!ParseBuildArgs(Args("Bed|dist|-1"), a, err), "negative dist refused");
  Check(!ParseBuildArgs(Args("Bed|dist|500"), a, err), "dist above 200 refused");
  Check(!ParseBuildArgs(Args("Bed|near"), a, err), "near without a name refused");
  Check(!ParseBuildArgs(Args("Bed|rotate|90"), a, err) &&
            err.find("rotate") != std::string::npos,
        "unknown option refused");

  // KAH 12: buildings that crash the game are refused.
  Check(BuildDeniedSid("43875-Newwworld.mod"), "Biofuel Distillery sid denied");
  Check(!BuildDeniedSid("1855-gamedata.base"), "Battery Bank sid allowed");
  Check(StorageWithoutInventory(true, 2, true, false), "storage without inventory refused");
  Check(!StorageWithoutInventory(true, 2, true, true), "storage with inventory allowed");
  Check(!StorageWithoutInventory(true, 4, true, false),
        "production building without inventory allowed (Grain Silo)");
  Check(!StorageWithoutInventory(true, 23, true, false),
        "battery without inventory allowed (Battery Bank)");
  Check(!StorageWithoutInventory(false, 0, true, false), "no functionality allowed");
  Check(!StorageWithoutInventory(true, 2, false, false), "unknown has-inventory allowed");

  printf(g_failed ? "%d FAILED\n" : "all build argument tests passed\n", g_failed);
  return g_failed ? 1 : 0;
}
```

`tests/character_ref_test.cpp`:

```cpp
// Offline test of #serial / #serial/index references (src/CharacterRef.h).
// Build and run: tests\run_tests.bat
#include "CharacterRef.h"

int g_failed = 0;
void Check(bool cond, const char *what) {
  printf("%s %s\n", cond ? "PASS" : "FAIL", what);
  if (!cond)
    ++g_failed;
}

int main() {
  CharacterRef r = ParseCharacterRef("Hungry Bandit");
  Check(!r.isRef, "a name is no reference");
  r = ParseCharacterRef("#374267584");
  Check(r.isRef && r.valid && !r.hasIndex && r.serial == 374267584UL, "#serial");
  r = ParseCharacterRef("#374267584/1234");
  Check(r.isRef && r.valid && r.hasIndex && r.serial == 374267584UL && r.index == 1234UL,
        "#serial/index");
  Check(!ParseCharacterRef("#").valid, "# alone is malformed");
  Check(!ParseCharacterRef("#12a").valid, "letters are malformed");
  Check(!ParseCharacterRef("#12/").valid, "missing index is malformed");
  Check(!ParseCharacterRef("#/12").valid, "missing serial is malformed");
  Check(!ParseCharacterRef("#1/2/3").valid, "two slashes are malformed");
  Check(!ParseCharacterRef("#-5").valid, "negative is malformed");
  Check(!ParseCharacterRef("#12345678901").valid, "too long is malformed");

  const std::string printed = FormatCharacterRef(374267584UL, 1234UL);
  Check(printed == "#374267584/1234", "printed form: serial first, then index");
  r = ParseCharacterRef(printed);
  Check(r.valid && r.hasIndex && r.serial == 374267584UL && r.index == 1234UL,
        "the printed form parses back to the same handle");

  printf(g_failed ? "%d FAILED\n" : "all character reference tests passed\n", g_failed);
  return g_failed ? 1 : 0;
}
```

`tests/extensions_test.cpp`:

```cpp
// Offline test of the extension registry (src/Extensions.cpp) with the game
// parts stubbed: registration rules, ok/error replies, deferred replies.
// Build and run: tests\run_tests.bat
#include "Harness.h"
#include "../include/KenshiAutomationHarness.h"

#include <cstdio>
#include <map>

// --- stubs for what Extensions.cpp uses from the rest of the DLL ---
std::vector<std::string> g_log;
std::map<std::string, std::string> g_outbox; // id -> "ok|error detail"
void Log(const std::string &msg) { g_log.push_back(msg); }
void WriteOutbox(const std::string &id, bool ok, const std::string &detail) {
  g_outbox[id] = std::string(ok ? "ok " : "error ") + detail;
}
std::string Lower(const std::string &value) {
  std::string out = value;
  for (size_t i = 0; i < out.size(); ++i)
    out[i] = (char)tolower((unsigned char)out[i]);
  return out;
}
bool IsBuiltinCommand(const std::string &name) { return Lower(name) == "status"; }

extern "C" {
int KAH_ApiVersion();
int KAH_RegisterCommand(const char *, const char *, KAH_CommandFn, void *);
int KAH_RegisterBeforeAttack(KAH_AttackFn, void *);
void KAH_Complete(const char *, int, const char *);
}

int g_failed = 0;
void Check(bool cond, const char *what) {
  printf("%s %s\n", cond ? "PASS" : "FAIL", what);
  if (!cond)
    ++g_failed;
}

std::vector<std::string> Line(const char *id, const char *cmd, const char *arg) {
  std::vector<std::string> f;
  f.push_back(id);
  f.push_back(cmd);
  if (arg)
    f.push_back(arg);
  return f;
}

int Echo(const char *, int argc, const char *const *argv, KAH_Reply *reply, void *) {
  if (argc < 2) {
    reply->append(reply, "usage: echo <text>");
    return KAH_ERROR;
  }
  reply->append(reply, "echo:");
  reply->append(reply, argv[1]);
  return KAH_OK;
}

std::string g_deferredId;
int Deferred(const char *id, int, const char *const *, KAH_Reply *, void *) {
  g_deferredId = id;
  return KAH_PENDING;
}

int Throws(const char *, int, const char *const *, KAH_Reply *, void *) { throw 1; }

int g_attacks = 0;
void OnAttack(void *, void *, void *user) { g_attacks += *(int *)user; }

int main() {
  Check(KAH_ApiVersion() == KAH_API_VERSION, "api version");
  Check(KAH_RegisterCommand("echo", "echo <text>", &Echo, nullptr) == 1, "register echo");
  Check(KAH_RegisterCommand("ECHO", "", &Echo, nullptr) == 0, "duplicate name refused");
  Check(KAH_RegisterCommand("status", "", &Echo, nullptr) == 0, "built-in name refused");
  Check(KAH_RegisterCommand("bad name", "", &Echo, nullptr) == 0, "invalid name refused");
  Check(KAH_RegisterCommand("nofn", "", nullptr, nullptr) == 0, "null handler refused");
  Check(KAH_RegisterCommand("later", "later", &Deferred, nullptr) == 1, "register later");
  Check(KAH_RegisterCommand("boom", "", &Throws, nullptr) == 1, "register boom");
  Check(HasExtensionCommand("echo") && !HasExtensionCommand("nope"), "lookup");

  bool ok = false, pending = false;
  std::string text = RunExtensionCommand(Line("1", "Echo", "hi"), ok, pending);
  Check(ok && !pending && text == "echo:hi", "ok reply (case-insensitive name)");
  text = RunExtensionCommand(Line("2", "echo", nullptr), ok, pending);
  Check(!ok && !pending && text == "usage: echo <text>", "error reply");
  text = RunExtensionCommand(Line("3", "boom", nullptr), ok, pending);
  Check(!ok && !pending && text.find("exception") != std::string::npos, "exception caught");

  text = RunExtensionCommand(Line("4", "later", nullptr), ok, pending);
  Check(pending && g_deferredId == "4" && g_outbox.count("4") == 0, "pending: no reply yet");
  KAH_Complete("4", KAH_OK, "done later");
  Check(g_outbox["4"] == "ok done later", "complete writes the reply");
  KAH_Complete("4", KAH_OK, "twice");
  Check(g_outbox["4"] == "ok done later", "second complete ignored");
  KAH_Complete("unknown", KAH_OK, "x");
  Check(g_outbox.count("unknown") == 0, "unknown id ignored");

  int weight = 2;
  Check(KAH_RegisterBeforeAttack(&OnAttack, &weight) == 1, "register attack hook");
  RunBeforeAttack(nullptr, nullptr);
  Check(g_attacks == 2, "attack hook called");
  Check(ExtensionCommandList().find("echo <text>") != std::string::npos, "help lists usage");

  printf(g_failed ? "%d FAILED\n" : "all passed\n", g_failed);
  return g_failed ? 1 : 0;
}
```

`tests/frame_stats_test.cpp`:

```cpp
// Offline test of the fps accumulator (src/FrameStats.h).
// Build and run: tests\run_tests.bat
#include "FrameStats.h"

#include <cmath>

int g_failed = 0;
void Check(bool cond, const char *what) {
  printf("%s %s\n", cond ? "PASS" : "FAIL", what);
  if (!cond)
    ++g_failed;
}
bool Near(double a, double b) { return fabs(a - b) < 1e-6; }

int main() {
  FrameStats s;
  Check(s.frames == 0 && s.AverageFps() == 0.0 && s.MinFps() == 0.0, "empty: zeros");
  Check(s.Report() == "avg=0.0 min=0.0 worst_ms=0.0 frames=0 seconds=0.0", "empty report");

  for (int i = 0; i < 59; ++i)
    s.Add(1.0 / 60.0);
  s.Add(0.05); // one 50 ms hitch
  Check(s.frames == 60, "60 frames counted");
  Check(Near(s.seconds, 59.0 / 60.0 + 0.05), "seconds = sum of deltas");
  Check(Near(s.AverageFps(), 60.0 / (59.0 / 60.0 + 0.05)), "average = frames / seconds");
  Check(Near(s.MinFps(), 20.0), "min fps from the worst frame");
  Check(s.Report() == "avg=58.1 min=20.0 worst_ms=50.0 frames=60 seconds=1.0", "report format");

  s.Add(0.0);
  s.Add(-1.0);
  s.Add(7200.0);
  Check(s.frames == 60, "zero, negative and huge deltas ignored");

  s.Reset();
  Check(s.frames == 0 && s.seconds == 0.0 && s.worstSec == 0.0, "reset clears the window");
  s.Add(0.02);
  Check(Near(s.AverageFps(), 50.0) && Near(s.MinFps(), 50.0), "new window after reset");

  printf(g_failed ? "%d FAILED\n" : "all fps tests passed\n", g_failed);
  return g_failed ? 1 : 0;
}
```

```cpp
// Offline test of the import flag names (src/ImportFlags.h).
// Build and run: tests\run_tests.bat
#include "ImportFlags.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cctype>
#include <cstdio>

std::string Lower(const std::string &value) {
  std::string out = value;
  std::transform(out.begin(), out.end(), out.begin(), ::tolower);
  return out;
}

int g_failed = 0;
void Check(bool cond, const char *what) {
  printf("%s %s\n", cond ? "PASS" : "FAIL", what);
  if (!cond)
    ++g_failed;
}

int main() {
  std::string bad;
  Check(ParseImportFlags("squad", bad) == 0x2, "squad");
  Check(ParseImportFlags("Squad,Buildings", bad) == 0x6, "two names, any case");
  Check(ParseImportFlags("all", bad) == 0x3E, "all = everything but reset");
  Check(ParseImportFlags("all,reset", bad) == 0x3F, "all plus reset");
  Check(ParseImportFlags("research,npcs,relations", bad) == 0x38, "research, npcs, relations");
  Check(ParseImportFlags("squad,,buildings", bad) == 0x6, "empty item skipped");
  Check(ParseImportFlags("squad,horses", bad) == -1 && bad == "horses", "unknown name refused");
  Check(ParseImportFlags("", bad) == -1, "empty list refused");
  Check(ImportFlagNames(0x3E) == "squad,buildings,research,npcs,relations", "names of all");
  Check(ImportFlagNames(0x1) == "reset", "name of reset");
  Check(ImportFlagNames(0) == "none", "no flags");
  printf(g_failed ? "%d FAILED\n" : "all import flag tests passed\n", g_failed);
  return g_failed ? 1 : 0;
}
```

`tests/kah_inbox_test.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Offline test of the inbox protocol (KAH 9): several kah.py clients send at
once while a fake harness consumes inbox.txt the way Plugin.cpp does
(rename to inbox.txt.reading, read, delete, append replies). Every command
must be answered exactly once. Run: python tests/kah_inbox_test.py"""
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import threading
import time

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
KAH = os.path.join(HERE, '..', 'client', 'kah.py')
CLIENTS, PER_CLIENT = 4, 15


def fake_harness(d, stop, seen):
    inbox, taken = os.path.join(d, 'inbox.txt'), os.path.join(d, 'inbox.txt.reading')
    outbox = os.path.join(d, 'outbox.txt')
    while not stop.is_set():
        if os.path.exists(inbox):
            try:
                os.replace(inbox, taken)
            except OSError:
                time.sleep(0.01)
                continue
            with open(taken, encoding='utf-8') as f:
                lines = [l.rstrip('\n') for l in f if l.strip()]
            os.remove(taken)
            with open(outbox, 'a', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n') as f:
                for line in lines:
                    fields = line.split('\t')
                    seen.append(fields[0])
                    f.write('%s\tok\techo %s\n' % (fields[0], ' '.join(fields[1:])))
        time.sleep(0.02)


def client(d, n, results):
    for i in range(PER_CLIENT):
        r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, KAH, '--dir', d, 'status', 'c%d' % n, str(i)],
                           capture_output=True, text=True)
        results.append((n, i, r.returncode, r.stdout.strip() + r.stderr.strip()))


def main():
    d = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='kah_inbox_')
    failed = 0
    try:
        open(os.path.join(d, 'enabled.flag'), 'w').close()
        stop, seen, results = threading.Event(), [], []
        h = threading.Thread(target=fake_harness, args=(d, stop, seen))
        h.start()
        threads = [threading.Thread(target=client, args=(d, n, results)) for n in range(CLIENTS)]
        for t in threads:
            t.start()
        for t in threads:
            t.join()
        stop.set()
        h.join()
        bad = [r for r in results if r[2] != 0 or ('echo status c%d %d' % (r[0], r[1])) not in r[3]]
        for r in bad[:5]:
            print('FAIL client %d cmd %d: exit %d %s' % r)
        total = CLIENTS * PER_CLIENT
        ok = not bad and len(results) == total
        print('%s every concurrent command answered (%d/%d)' % ('PASS' if ok else 'FAIL',
                                                                 len(results) - len(bad), total))
        failed += not ok
        ok = len(seen) == total and len(set(seen)) == total
        print('%s each command reached the harness once, ids unique (%d seen, %d unique)' %
              ('PASS' if ok else 'FAIL', len(seen), len(set(seen))))
        failed += not ok
        left = [n for n in os.listdir(d) if n.startswith('inbox')]
        print('%s no inbox, lock or temp files left (%s)' % ('PASS' if not left else 'FAIL', left))
        failed += bool(left)
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(d, ignore_errors=True)
    print('%d FAILED' % failed if failed else 'all inbox protocol tests passed')
    return 1 if failed else 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())

```

`tests/production_counter_test.cpp`:

```cpp
// Offline test of the "produced" counter (src/ProductionCounter.h).
// Build and run: tests\run_tests.bat
#include "ProductionCounter.h"

#include <string>

#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>

int g_failed = 0;
void Check(bool cond, const char *what) {
  printf("%s %s\n", cond ? "PASS" : "FAIL", what);
  if (!cond)
    ++g_failed;
}

int main() {
  ProductionCounter c;
  c.Reset(10.0);
  c.Sample(5); // first sample: baseline only
  Check(c.produced == 0 && c.removed == 0, "first sample is the baseline");
  c.Sample(6);
  c.Sample(7);
  c.Sample(7);
  Check(c.produced == 2, "rises count as produced");
  c.Sample(0); // a worker hauls everything
  Check(c.produced == 2 && c.removed == 7, "hauling counts as removed, not hiding output");
  c.Sample(1);
  c.Sample(3); // fast game speed: two units in one frame
  Check(c.produced == 5, "production after hauling and multi-unit steps");
  c.Sample(-1);
  Check(c.produced == 5 && c.last == 3, "negative quantity ignored");
  Check(fabs(c.Hours(12.5) - 2.5) < 1e-9, "game hours since start");
  Check(fabs(c.PerGameHour(12.5) - 2.0) < 1e-9, "units per game hour");
  Check(c.PerGameHour(10.0) == 0.0 && c.Hours(9.0) == 0.0, "no time passed: rate 0");

  c.ResetKeepLevel(20.0);
  Check(c.produced == 0 && c.removed == 0 && c.startHours == 20.0, "reset clears counts");
  c.Sample(4);
  Check(c.produced == 1, "after a reset counting continues from the last level (3 -> 4)");

  ProductionCounter fresh;
  fresh.ResetKeepLevel(1.0);
  fresh.Sample(9);
  Check(fresh.produced == 0, "reset before any sample: next sample is the baseline");

  Check(c.samples == 1, "samples counted since the reset");

  // Two consecutive "produced" calls for the same building must hit the
  // same entry (run m13: every call made a new one).
  struct Entry {
    ProductionKey key;
    std::string name;
  };
  std::vector<Entry> table;
  Entry mine = {ProductionKey(1234, 374267584), "Stone Mine"};
  Check(FindProductionEntry(table, mine.key) == -1, "unknown building: no entry");
  table.push_back(mine);
  Entry other = {ProductionKey(1235, 99), "Iron Refinery"};
  table.push_back(other);
  Check(FindProductionEntry(table, ProductionKey(1234, 374267584)) == 0, "first call's key found again");
  Check(FindProductionEntry(table, ProductionKey(1234, 374267584)) == 0, "second call hits the same entry");
  Check(FindProductionEntry(table, ProductionKey(1235, 99)) == 1, "other building, own entry");
  Check(FindProductionEntry(table, ProductionKey(1234, 1)) == -1, "same index, new serial: a different building");

  printf(g_failed ? "%d FAILED\n" : "all production counter tests passed\n", g_failed);
  return g_failed ? 1 : 0;
}
```

`tests/search_radius_test.cpp`:

```cpp
// Offline test of the search radius arguments (src/SearchRadius.h) used by
// building, buildings, benches, power, fill, job and teleport ... building.
// Build and run: tests\run_tests.bat
#include "SearchRadius.h"

#include <cctype>
#include <cstdio>
#include <sstream>

std::string Lower(const std::string &value) {
  std::string out = value;
  for (size_t i = 0; i < out.size(); ++i)
    out[i] = (char)tolower((unsigned char)out[i]);
  return out;
}
std::string Num(double v) {
  std::ostringstream s;
  s << v;
  return s.str();
}

int g_failed = 0;
void Check(bool cond, const char *what) {
  printf("%s %s\n", cond ? "PASS" : "FAIL", what);
  if (!cond)
    ++g_failed;
}

// f = id, command, then the arguments.
std::vector<std::string> Args(const char *a, const char *b = 0, const char *c = 0,
                              const char *d = 0, const char *e = 0, const char *g = 0) {
  std::vector<std::string> f;
  f.push_back("1");
  const char *all[] = {a, b, c, d, e, g};
  for (int i = 0; i < 6 && all[i]; ++i)
    f.push_back(all[i]);
  return f;
}

int main() {
  float r = 0;
  std::string err;

  Check(ParseSearchRadius(Args("building", "Stone Mine"), 3, 3, 300.0f, r, err) && r == 300.0f,
        "no radius -> default");
  Check(ParseSearchRadius(Args("building", "Stone Mine", "800"), 3, 3, 300.0f, r, err) &&
            r == 800.0f,
        "positional radius");
  Check(ParseSearchRadius(Args("building", "Stone Mine", "radius", "750.5"), 3, 3, 300.0f, r,
                          err) && r == 750.5f,
        "keyword radius");
  Check(ParseSearchRadius(Args("job", "Shay", "Stone Mine", "task", "OPERATE_MACHINERY"), 4, 0,
                          300.0f, r, err) && r == 300.0f,
        "job with task, no radius");
  Check(ParseSearchRadius(Args("job", "Shay", "Stone Mine", "radius", "900"), 4, 0, 300.0f, r,
                          err) && r == 900.0f,
        "job radius keyword");
  Check(ParseSearchRadius(Args("fill", "Generator", "Iron Plates", "3"), 4, 0, 300.0f, r, err) &&
            r == 300.0f,
        "fill count is not a radius (no positional form)");
  Check(ParseSearchRadius(Args("benches", "crafts"), 2, 2, 300.0f, r, err) && r == 300.0f,
        "benches crafts -> default");
  Check(ParseSearchRadius(Args("benches", "1200", "crafts"), 2, 2, 300.0f, r, err) && r == 1200.0f,
        "benches positional radius");
  Check(ParseSearchRadius(Args("buildings", "100", "radius"), 3, 2, 100.0f, r, err) &&
            r == 100.0f,
        "buildings: 'radius' as a filter word is no keyword without a value");
  Check(ParseSearchRadius(Args("building", "Mine", "5000"), 3, 3, 300.0f, r, err) && r == 5000.0f,
        "maximum allowed");

  err.clear();
  Check(!ParseSearchRadius(Args("building", "Mine", "5001"), 3, 3, 300.0f, r, err) &&
            err.find("5000") != std::string::npos,
        "above maximum refused");
  Check(!ParseSearchRadius(Args("building", "Mine", "radius", "0"), 3, 3, 300.0f, r, err),
        "zero refused");
  Check(!ParseSearchRadius(Args("building", "Mine", "radius", "-5"), 3, 3, 300.0f, r, err),
        "negative refused");
  Check(!ParseSearchRadius(Args("job", "Shay", "Mine", "RADIUS", "far"), 4, 0, 300.0f, r, err),
        "non-number refused (keyword is case-insensitive)");

  Check(ParseSearchRadius(Args("shopstock", "Abia"), 3, 0, kDefaultShopRadius, r, err,
                          kMaxShopRadius) && r == 60.0f,
        "shopstock default 60");
  Check(ParseSearchRadius(Args("trade", "Shay", "Abia", "Bandage", "radius"), 5, 0,
                          kDefaultShopRadius, r, err, kMaxShopRadius) && r == 60.0f,
        "trade: trailing 'radius' without a value is ignored");
  Check(ParseSearchRadius(Args("trade", "Shay", "Abia", "Bandage", "radius", "120"), 5, 0,
                          kDefaultShopRadius, r, err, kMaxShopRadius) && r == 120.0f,
        "trade radius keyword");
  Check(!ParseSearchRadius(Args("shopstock", "Abia", "radius", "301"), 3, 0, kDefaultShopRadius,
                           r, err, kMaxShopRadius) && err.find("300") != std::string::npos,
        "shop radius above 300 refused");

  printf(g_failed ? "%d FAILED\n" : "all search radius tests passed\n", g_failed);
  return g_failed ? 1 : 0;
}
```

`tools/Win32Ui.cs`:

```cs
// Win32 helpers for the Kenshi test automation (loaded with Add-Type).
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Text;

public static class KenshiWin32 {
  public delegate bool EnumProc(IntPtr hwnd, IntPtr lParam);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool EnumWindows(EnumProc cb, IntPtr lParam);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool EnumChildWindows(IntPtr parent, EnumProc cb, IntPtr lParam);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern uint GetWindowThreadProcessId(IntPtr hwnd, out uint pid);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool IsWindowVisible(IntPtr hwnd);
  [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern int GetClassName(IntPtr hwnd, StringBuilder sb, int max);
  [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern int GetWindowText(IntPtr hwnd, StringBuilder sb, int max);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern int GetDlgCtrlID(IntPtr hwnd);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern IntPtr SendMessage(IntPtr hwnd, uint msg, IntPtr w, IntPtr l);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool PostMessage(IntPtr hwnd, uint msg, IntPtr w, IntPtr l);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool GetWindowRect(IntPtr hwnd, out RECT r);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(IntPtr hwnd);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern IntPtr GetForegroundWindow();
  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] public struct RECT { public int Left, Top, Right, Bottom; }

  public const uint WM_COMMAND = 0x0111;
  public const uint BM_CLICK = 0x00F5;

  public class Win {
    public IntPtr Handle; public uint Pid; public string Cls; public string Title; public int Id;
    public override string ToString() {
      return string.Format("0x{0:X} pid={1} id={2} class=[{3}] title=[{4}]", Handle.ToInt64(), Pid, Id, Cls, Title);
    }
  }

  static Win Describe(IntPtr h) {
    var c = new StringBuilder(256); var t = new StringBuilder(512);
    GetClassName(h, c, 256); GetWindowText(h, t, 512);
    uint pid; GetWindowThreadProcessId(h, out pid);
    return new Win { Handle = h, Pid = pid, Cls = c.ToString(), Title = t.ToString(), Id = GetDlgCtrlID(h) };
  }

  // Visible top-level windows owned by any of the given process ids.
  public static List<Win> TopWindows(uint[] pids) {
    var set = new HashSet<uint>(pids); var list = new List<Win>();
    EnumWindows((h, l) => {
      uint pid; GetWindowThreadProcessId(h, out pid);
      if (set.Contains(pid) && IsWindowVisible(h)) list.Add(Describe(h));
      return true;
    }, IntPtr.Zero);
    return list;
  }

  public static List<Win> Children(IntPtr parent) {
    var list = new List<Win>();
    EnumChildWindows(parent, (h, l) => { list.Add(Describe(h)); return true; }, IntPtr.Zero);
    return list;
  }

  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern void keybd_event(byte vk, byte scan, uint flags, UIntPtr extra);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool ShowWindow(IntPtr hwnd, int cmd);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool IsIconic(IntPtr hwnd);

  // Windows only lets the foreground app hand focus away; a synthetic Alt
  // tap lifts that lock so a background script can focus the game window.
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool AttachThreadInput(uint idAttach, uint idAttachTo, bool attach);
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] public static extern uint GetCurrentThreadId();
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool BringWindowToTop(IntPtr hwnd);

  public static bool ForceForeground(IntPtr hwnd) {
    if (IsIconic(hwnd)) ShowWindow(hwnd, 9); // SW_RESTORE
    keybd_event(0x12, 0, 0, UIntPtr.Zero);      // Alt down
    keybd_event(0x12, 0, 2, UIntPtr.Zero);      // Alt up
    if (SetForegroundWindow(hwnd) && GetForegroundWindow() == hwnd) return true;
    // Fallback: share input state with the current foreground thread.
    uint pid;
    uint fgThread = GetWindowThreadProcessId(GetForegroundWindow(), out pid);
    uint me = GetCurrentThreadId();
    AttachThreadInput(me, fgThread, true);
    BringWindowToTop(hwnd);
    SetForegroundWindow(hwnd);
    AttachThreadInput(me, fgThread, false);
    return GetForegroundWindow() == hwnd;
  }

  // Presses a dialog button the way a click would (WM_COMMAND to the dialog).
  public static void PressDialogButton(IntPtr dialog, int id) {
    PostMessage(dialog, WM_COMMAND, new IntPtr(id), IntPtr.Zero);
  }
}

```

`tools/WindowCapture.cs`:

```cs
// Screenshot of one window, also when it is behind other windows
// (PrintWindow with PW_RENDERFULLCONTENT works for D3D11 windows on Windows 10).
using System;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Drawing.Imaging;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;

public static class KenshiCapture {
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] static extern bool PrintWindow(IntPtr hwnd, IntPtr hdc, uint flags);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] static extern bool GetClientRect(IntPtr hwnd, out RECT r);
  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] struct RECT { public int Left, Top, Right, Bottom; }

  // Saves a PNG scaled to at most maxWidth pixels wide; returns "w x h".
  public static string Save(IntPtr hwnd, string path, int maxWidth) {
    RECT r;
    GetClientRect(hwnd, out r);
    int w = Math.Max(1, r.Right - r.Left), h = Math.Max(1, r.Bottom - r.Top);
    using (var bmp = new Bitmap(w, h, PixelFormat.Format32bppArgb)) {
      using (var g = Graphics.FromImage(bmp)) {
        IntPtr hdc = g.GetHdc();
        PrintWindow(hwnd, hdc, 1 | 2); // PW_CLIENTONLY | PW_RENDERFULLCONTENT
        g.ReleaseHdc(hdc);
      }
      int ow = Math.Min(w, maxWidth), oh = (int)((long)h * ow / w);
      using (var scaled = new Bitmap(bmp, ow, oh)) scaled.Save(path, ImageFormat.Png);
      return ow + "x" + oh;
    }
  }
}

```

`tools/kenshi-ctl.ps1`:

```ps1
<#
kenshi-ctl.ps1: start, stop and watch Kenshi for automated test runs.

  kenshi-ctl.ps1 status                 running? pid, uptime, window, launcher open?
  kenshi-ctl.ps1 launch [-Save <name>]  archive logs, start Kenshi, press the launcher's OK,
                                        wait for the harness (and load <name> if given)
  kenshi-ctl.ps1 stop                   kill Kenshi (never saves)
  kenshi-ctl.ps1 restart [-Save <name>] stop + launch
  kenshi-ctl.ps1 health                 ok | crashed | hung | not-running (exit code 0/1)
  kenshi-ctl.ps1 screenshot [-Save n]   PNG of the game window (works in the background)
                                        -> <ArchiveRoot>\shots\<n|time>.png

Options: -Kenshi <game folder> (default: $env:KENSHI_DIR, else the Steam default),
-ArchiveRoot <folder> (default C:\KenshiTestRuns), -ExtraLogs <files> (other mods'
logs to archive before each launch, e.g. because they reset on launch).

Steam must be running (steam_appid.txt next to kenshi_x64.exe lets the exe
start without Steam's launch prompt). With RE_Kenshi the game restarts into
RE_Kenshi\kenshi_x64.exe --norestart; the launcher dialog opens in that second
process, so both are followed. Loading a save needs the harness enabled
(kah on, i.e. mods\AutomationHarness\enabled.flag).
#>
param(
  [Parameter(Position = 0)][string]$Command = 'status',
  [string]$Save = '',
  [int]$TimeoutSec = 300,
  [string]$Kenshi = $(if ($env:KENSHI_DIR) { $env:KENSHI_DIR } else { 'C:\Program Files (x86)\Steam\steamapps\common\Kenshi' }),
  [string]$ArchiveRoot = 'C:\KenshiTestRuns',
  [string[]]$ExtraLogs = @()
)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
Add-Type -Path (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'Win32Ui.cs')

$HarnessDir = "$Kenshi\mods\AutomationHarness"
$HarnessLog = "$HarnessDir\harness.log"
$LogArchive = Join-Path $ArchiveRoot 'logs'
$LauncherOkId = 1003

if (-not (Test-Path "$Kenshi\kenshi_x64.exe")) { throw "no kenshi_x64.exe in $Kenshi (use -Kenshi or KENSHI_DIR)" }

# The leading comma keeps a one-process result an array (so .Count works).
function Get-KenshiProcs { , @(Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter "Name='kenshi_x64.exe'") }

function Get-Launcher {
  $procs = Get-KenshiProcs
  if ($procs.Count -eq 0) { return $null }
  $pids = [uint32[]]@($procs | ForEach-Object { [uint32]$_.ProcessId })
  [KenshiWin32]::TopWindows($pids) | Where-Object { $_.Cls -eq '#32770' -and $_.Title -like 'Kenshi*' } | Select-Object -First 1
}

function Get-GameWindow {
  $procs = Get-KenshiProcs
  if ($procs.Count -eq 0) { return $null }
  $pids = [uint32[]]@($procs | ForEach-Object { [uint32]$_.ProcessId })
  [KenshiWin32]::TopWindows($pids) | Where-Object { $_.Cls -like 'OgreD3D*' } | Select-Object -First 1
}

function Get-LatestCrashDump {
  Get-ChildItem $Kenshi -Filter 'crashDump*.zip' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
    Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1
}

function Save-Logs([string]$Reason) {
  $stamp = Get-Date -Format 'yyyyMMdd-HHmmss'
  $dest = Join-Path $LogArchive "$stamp-$Reason"
  New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $dest | Out-Null
  foreach ($f in @($HarnessLog, "$HarnessDir\outbox.txt", "$Kenshi\RE_Kenshi_log.txt") + $ExtraLogs) {
    if (Test-Path $f) { Copy-Item $f $dest -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }
  }
  $dest
}

function Stop-Kenshi {
  $procs = Get-KenshiProcs
  foreach ($p in $procs) { Stop-Process -Id $p.ProcessId -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }
  $deadline = (Get-Date).AddSeconds(30)
  while ((Get-KenshiProcs).Count -gt 0 -and (Get-Date) -lt $deadline) { Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500 }
  if ((Get-KenshiProcs).Count -gt 0) { throw 'Kenshi did not exit' }
  "stopped ($($procs.Count) process(es))"
}

function Wait-Until([scriptblock]$Cond, [int]$Seconds, [string]$What) {
  $deadline = (Get-Date).AddSeconds($Seconds)
  while ((Get-Date) -lt $deadline) {
    $r = & $Cond
    if ($r) { return $r }
    if ((Get-KenshiProcs).Count -eq 0) { throw "Kenshi exited while waiting for $What" }
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500
  }
  throw "timed out after $Seconds s waiting for $What"
}

function Read-HarnessLogMatch([string]$Pattern) {
  if (-not (Test-Path $HarnessLog)) { return $null }
  Select-String -Path $HarnessLog -Pattern $Pattern -SimpleMatch | Select-Object -Last 1
}

function Start-Kenshi {
  if ((Get-KenshiProcs).Count -gt 0) { throw 'Kenshi is already running (stop it first)' }
  if (-not (Get-Process steam -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) { throw 'Steam is not running' }
  if (-not (Test-Path "$HarnessDir\AutomationHarness.dll")) { throw "harness not installed in $HarnessDir" }
  $archived = Save-Logs 'before-launch'
  "logs archived to $archived"
  # The harness recreates harness.log on start; removing it keeps the waits
  # below from matching lines of the previous session.
  Remove-Item $HarnessLog -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  if ($Save) {
    if (-not (Test-Path "$HarnessDir\enabled.flag")) { throw 'harness is off (kah on) so autoload would be ignored' }
    Set-Content -Path "$HarnessDir\autoload.txt" -Value $Save -Encoding Ascii -NoNewline
  }
  Remove-Item "$HarnessDir\inbox.txt", "$HarnessDir\inbox.txt.lock", "$HarnessDir\inbox.txt.reading" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  $t0 = Get-Date
  Start-Process -FilePath "$Kenshi\kenshi_x64.exe" -WorkingDirectory $Kenshi | Out-Null
  $launcher = Wait-Until { Get-Launcher } 120 'the launcher dialog'
  # Let the dialog finish initialising (mod list) before pressing OK.
  Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
  [KenshiWin32]::PressDialogButton($launcher.Handle, $LauncherOkId)
  "launcher OK pressed after $([int]((Get-Date) - $t0).TotalSeconds) s"
  Wait-Until { -not (Get-Launcher) -and (Get-GameWindow) } 60 'the game window' | Out-Null
  Wait-Until { Read-HarnessLogMatch 'KAH: frame listener running' } $TimeoutSec 'the harness frame listener' | Out-Null
  "frame listener running after $([int]((Get-Date) - $t0).TotalSeconds) s"
  if ($Save) {
    Wait-Until { Read-HarnessLogMatch "KAH: autoload $Save" } $TimeoutSec 'autoload' | ForEach-Object { $_.Line }
  }
  $p = Get-KenshiProcs | Select-Object -First 1
  "running: pid=$($p.ProcessId) cmd=$($p.CommandLine)"
}

function Get-Health {
  $procs = Get-KenshiProcs
  if ($procs.Count -eq 0) {
    $dump = Get-LatestCrashDump
    if ($dump -and $dump.LastWriteTime -gt (Get-Date).AddMinutes(-10)) { return "crashed (dump $($dump.Name) at $($dump.LastWriteTime))" }
    return 'not-running'
  }
  $win = Get-GameWindow
  if (-not $win) { if (Get-Launcher) { return 'launcher-open' } return 'no-window' }
  $p = Get-Process -Id $procs[0].ProcessId
  if (-not $p.Responding) { return 'hung (window not responding)' }
  return 'ok'
}

switch ($Command) {
  'status' {
    $procs = Get-KenshiProcs
    if ($procs.Count -eq 0) { 'not running'; break }
    foreach ($p in $procs) { "pid=$($p.ProcessId) started=$($p.CreationDate) cmd=$($p.CommandLine)" }
    "launcher open: $([bool](Get-Launcher))   game window: $([bool](Get-GameWindow))   health: $(Get-Health)"
  }
  'launch' { Start-Kenshi }
  'stop' { Save-Logs 'stop' | Out-Null; Stop-Kenshi }
  'restart' { if ((Get-KenshiProcs).Count -gt 0) { Save-Logs 'restart' | Out-Null; Stop-Kenshi }; Start-Kenshi }
  'health' { $h = Get-Health; $h; if ($h -ne 'ok') { exit 1 } }
  'screenshot' {
    Add-Type -Path (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'WindowCapture.cs') -ReferencedAssemblies System.Drawing
    $win = Get-GameWindow
    if (-not $win) { throw 'no game window' }
    $dir = Join-Path $ArchiveRoot 'shots'
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $dir | Out-Null
    $path = if ($Save) { Join-Path $dir "$Save.png" } else { Join-Path $dir ("shot-" + (Get-Date -Format 'yyyyMMdd-HHmmss') + '.png') }
    $size = [KenshiCapture]::Save($win.Handle, $path, 1600)
    "$path ($size)"
  }
  default { throw "unknown command: $Command" }
}

```
